◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."
◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (17/10/6), with 4 wave-4 fought, 15 contests survived, 3 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices. Ladder — National Standing reached (Fight 3 · Forage 19 · Craft 9 · Talk 23 · Explore 44 → 24 · capped) (+4 feast-bundle bonus)
◈ For a full seat, the ledger still wants: only 0 of 2 different wave-5 monsters faced; no capstone deed delivered (military tribute, trade charter, or feast embassy).
◈ THE WAR PATH — Footage plays. The galaxy has already watched these fights twice. The System narrates anyway — it likes the sound of its own broadcast voice. On day 124, Ethan put down Big-Eyes.
"THE HERALD came. Named and located — the war council knew its face. They faced Big Ugly and lived to sit here. They faced The Loud Beast and lived to sit here. 1 more of the later drafts faced besides. Even now the wave-5 bar reads: blood your fighters (1 of 2 veterans)." — the System is still reading the list.
The table notes 1 ascension from one village — its terms improve accordingly. Nobody has ever seen that before.
The table notes the overtime: 1 week kept waiting, 2 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −10.
📺 SYSTEM: "EVERYONE. SIT. WATCH. The teeth and the weather can WAIT — nothing out there matters until this is done. ...Did I stutter? SIT."
◈ ABILITY — Spotlight: The room is already warm. Audience starts at 2.
◈ ABILITY — Callback: One bad act can be redone. (Offered once, after a bad beat.)
◈ ABILITY — Standing-O: Genuine love doesn't crater: the audience can't fall below 0 while you hold it.

◈ THE BELOVED — The audience is chanting. You don't know the words — it isn't a language with words — but you know the feeling. The table can't move against the cast's favorite. That's leverage. Tonight, the cast is your village.
The table has read your ledger. More promises broken than kept; the protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared. The light road is open.
Ivy tells the truth — the unproduced kind. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. You can't fake this. You didn't.
You play it the way a Cartographer plays everything: all the way, in public. The name holds.
You spend it all — the love, the light, the finale slot — on people the cameras never found. You name the unfilmed villages. You name the dead. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. This is what the show was for, it turns out.
◈ THE BELOVED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE BELOVED — "Let the audience speak."
The show becomes the government. Strange. It works. Trillions of viewers hold the table to account in real time, and humanity's seat is wherever the cameras are. You made popularity into a constitution — and constitutions, unlike ratings, don't dip.
You put the crossings on the map, and the hard climbs, and the places where somebody had offered water.
You put the landmarks where another hand could copy them. A map did not have to come with its maker.
◈ THE ARCHITECT — Ivan Villa ascended through doors you built: relay. The galaxy will remember the god's name. The village will remember who built the door. Both are true. Neither is small.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
The mourners asked you, and you changed the subject.
The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
💛 You hold Contract Quill. It has signed three leases, two loans, and one terrible contract you still regret. The penmanship was always excellent. The judgment, less so. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they know your hands. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Mashael's Drawing. Kitchen table, burnt pancakes. You told her the sun needed holding, and she took you literally — drew you carrying it with both hands. She was six. The sky changed three weeks later. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they have carried you this far. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Carpenter's square. Your first solo frame, checked and found true. The nod that followed was the whole ceremony. Measure twice, cut once — it's in your hands now. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they are still yours. The feast was the weapon.
